CStrings:
+ "%s didNavigateToNonRootPage — tip never shown, hiding temporarily"
+ "%s didNavigateToNonRootPage — tip was shown before, dismissing permanently"
+ "%s updateAccountButtonBubbleTipVisibility isVisible=%{bool}d"
+ ".apple.com"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchAndShowIfNeeded fetching %s"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchAndShowIfNeeded presenting from cache"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchAndShowIfNeeded skipped makeAnchor() returned nil"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchAndShowIfNeeded skipped playback UI being shown"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchAndShowIfNeeded skipped presented=%{bool}d fetchInFlight=%{bool}d userDismissed=%{bool}d"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchBubbleTip completed for %s but fetchInFlight already cleared, dropping"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::fetchBubbleTip returned nil for %s"
+ "AccountButtonBubbleTip::hideForTemporarySwitch dismissing"
